DISPATCHES_PER_YEAR_10\n        / UNITS_COST_DENOMINATOR\n    )\n\n    annual_drug_cost_20 = (\n        program.acquisition_cost_per_1000_units_usd\n        * sites_d\n        * program.units_per_day\n        * DAYS_SUPPLY_20\n        * DISPATCHES_PER_YEAR_20\n        / UNITS_COST_DENOMINATOR\n    )\n\n    annual_cooler_cost_10 = cooler_cost_usd * DISPATCHES_PER_YEAR_10\n    annual_cooler_cost_20 = cooler_cost_usd * DISPATCHES_PER_YEAR_20\n\n    annual_revenue_10 = payment_per_dispatch_per_site_usd * sites_d * DISPATCHES_PER_YEAR_10\n    annual_revenue_20 = payment_per_dispatch_per_site_usd * sites_d * DISPATCHES_PER_YEAR_20\n\n    annual_margin_10 = annual_revenue_10 - annual_drug_cost_10 - annual_cooler_cost_10\n    annual_margin_20 = annual_revenue_20 - annual_drug_cost_20 - annual_cooler_cost_20\n\n    diff_20_minus_10 = annual_margin_20 - annual_margin_10\n\n    # Currency rounding requirements (2 decimals).\n    annual_drug_cost_10_r = money2(annual_drug_cost_10)\n    annual_drug_cost_20_r = money2(annual_drug_cost_20)\n    annual_cooler_cost_10_r = money2(annual_cooler_cost_10)\n    annual_cooler_cost_20_r = money2(annual_cooler_cost_20)\n    annual_revenue_10_r = money2(annual_revenue_10)\n    annual_revenue_20_r = money2(annual_revenue_20)\n    annual_margin_10_r = money2(annual_margin_10)\n    annual_margin_20_r = money2(annual_margin_20)\n    diff_20_minus_10_r = money2(diff_20_minus_10)\n\n    return {\n        \"program_code\": program.program_code,\n        \"program_name\": program.program_name,\n        \"cooler_type\": program.cooler_type,\n        \"active_sites\": active_sites,\n        \"payment_per_dispatch_per_site_usd\": float(money2(payment_per_dispatch_per_site_usd)),\n        \"cooler_cost_usd\": float(money2(cooler_cost_usd)),\n        \"acquisition_cost_per_1000_units_usd\": float(money2(program.acquisition_cost_per_1000_units_usd)),\n        \"units_per_day\": float(program.units_per_day),\n        \"annual_drug_cost_10_day\": float(annual_drug_cost_10_r),\n        \"annual_drug_cost_20_day\": float(annual_drug_cost_20_r),\n        \"annual_cooler_cost_10_day\": float(annual_cooler_cost_10_r),\n        \"annual_cooler_cost_20_day\": float(annual_cooler_cost_20_r),\n        \"annual_revenue_10_day\": float(annual_revenue_10_r),\n        \"annual_revenue_20_day\": float(annual_revenue_20_r),\n        \"annual_margin_10_day\": float(annual_margin_10_r),\n        \"annual_margin_20_day\": float(annual_margin_20_r),\n        \"annual_margin_difference_20_minus_10\": float(diff_20_minus_10_r),\n    }\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    program_objs = load_program_catalog_as_list(PROGRAM_CATALOG_PATH)\n    programs_all = [to_program(p) for p in program_objs]\n\n    # 2b. Filter in-scope programs.\n    in_scope = [p for p in programs_all if p.review_flag == \"review\"]\n\n    cooler_rows = read_csv_dicts(COOLER_COST_PATH)\n    payment_rows = read_csv_dicts(CONTRACT_PAYMENT_PATH)\n    override_rows = read_csv_dicts(SITE_OVERRIDES_PATH)\n\n    payment_by_code = resolve_contract_payments(in_scope, payment_rows)\n    active_sites_by_code = resolve_active_sites(in_scope, override_rows)\n    cooler_cost_by_type = resolve_cooler_cost(cooler_rows)\n\n    programs_out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n\n    for p in in_scope:\n        if p.cooler_type not in cooler_cost_by_type:\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"No cooler_cost_usd found for cooler_type '{p.cooler_type}' (program {p.program_code})\"\n            )\n\n        active_sites = active_sites_by_code[p.program_code]\n        payment = payment_by_code[p.program_code]\n        cooler_cost = cooler_cost_by_type[p.cooler_type]\n\n        programs_out.append(\n            compute_program_financials(\n                program=p,\n                active_sites=active_sites,\n                payment_per_dispatch_per_site_usd=payment,\n                cooler_cost_usd=cooler_cost,\n            )\n        )\n\n    # Sort programs by program_code ascending.\n    programs_out.sort(key=lambda r: r[\"program_code\"])\n\n    # 2g. Totals.\n    total_margin_10 = money2(sum(_d(p[\"annual_margin_10_day\"]) for p in programs_out))\n    total_margin_20 = money2(sum(_d(p[\"annual_margin_20_day\"]) for p in programs_out))\n    total_diff = money2(sum(_d(p[\"annual_margin_difference_20_minus_10\"]) for p in programs_out))\n    abs_total_diff = money2(abs(total_diff))\n\n    # 2h. Decision.\n    decision = \"move_to_20_day\" if abs_total_diff < DECISION_THRESHOLD_ABS_USD else \"keep_10_day\"\n\n    # 2i. Output JSON (schema-oriented).\n    assumptions = {\n        \"days_supply_10_day\": int(DAYS_SUPPLY_10),\n        \"days_supply_20_day\": int(DAYS_SUPPLY_20),\n        \"dispatches_per_year_10_day\": int(DISPATCHES_PER_YEAR_10),\n        \"dispatches_per_year_20_day\": int(DISPATCHES_PER_YEAR_20),\n        \"units_cost_denominator\": int(UNITS_COST_DENOMINATOR),\n        \"decision_threshold_absolute_usd\": float(money2(DECISION_THRESHOLD_ABS_USD)),\n        \"active_sites_override_rule\": \"Use latest approved override by highest version_no; else default_active_sites\",\n        \"payment_matching_rule\": \"contract_payment.program_label matches program_name or known_labels for in-scope programs\",\n    }\n\n    justification = (\n        f\"Computed annual margins for {len(programs_out)} in-scope programs under 10-day (36 dispatches/year) \"\n        f\"and 20-day (18 dispatches/year) models. Annual drug volume is identical across models \"\n        f\"(10*36 == 20*18 == 360 days of therapy), so differences are driven by revenue and cooler dispatch counts. \"\n        f\"Total margin difference (20 minus 10) is {float(total_diff):.2f} USD (absolute {float(abs_total_diff):.2f} USD), \"\n        f\"so decision is '{decision}' using the 10000 USD threshold.\"\n    )\n\n    out_obj = {\n        \"decision\": decision,\n        \"totals\": {\n            \"total_annual_margin_10_day_usd\": float(total_margin_10),\n            \"total_annual_margin_20_day_usd\": float(total_margin_20),\n            \"total_annual_margin_difference_20_minus_10_usd\": float(total_diff),\n            \"absolute_total_margin_difference_usd\": float(abs_total_diff),\n        },\n        \"programs\": programs_out,\n        \"assumptions\": assumptions,\n        \"justification\": justification,\n    }\n\n    OUTPUT_JSON_PATH.write_text(json.dumps(out_obj, indent=2) + \"\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n    # 2j. Summary markdown (4-8 non-empty lines).\n    md_lines = [\n        \"# OncoCooler Dispatch Cadence Summary\",\n        f\"Total 10-day annual margin (USD): {float(total_margin_10):.2f}\",\n        f\"Total 20-day annual margin (USD): {float(total_margin_20):.2f}\",\n        f\"Absolute difference (USD): {float(abs_total_diff):.2f}\",\n        f\"Decision: {decision}\",\n    ]\n    OUTPUT_MD_PATH.write_text(\"\\n\".join(md_lines) + \"\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}
